+namespace {
+
+constexpr int CUDA_THREADS = 256;
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFail(const cudaError_t error, const char* operation,
+                           const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d during %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                        \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_error__ = (operation);                           \
+        if (cuda_error__ != cudaSuccess) {                                      \
+            cudaFail(cuda_error__, #operation, __FILE__, __LINE__);             \
+        }                                                                       \
+    } while (false)
+
+void initializeCudaRuntime() {
+    int device_count = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count));
+    if (device_count == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "QT clustering requires a CUDA-capable GPU.\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+    // Force primary-context creation before the benchmark timer starts.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));
+}
+
+// Every row represents the candidate cluster grown from one seed.  The
+// matrix is filled once and then turns each diameter update into one load.
+// Keeping distances as double precision Euclidean distances preserves the
+// comparisons performed by the sequential implementation.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrixKernel(const Point* const points,
+                                          double* const distances,
+                                          const int point_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t element_count = static_cast<size_t>(point_count) * point_count;
+    if (index >= element_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[row].x - points[column].x;
+    const double dy = points[row].y - points[column].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+// Initialize one independent candidate cluster for every currently
+// unclustered seed.  The member mask and maximum-distance vector are stored
+// row-major so that each warp accesses consecutive candidate points.
+__global__ void initializeCandidateStateKernel(const int* const seeds,
+                                                const double* const distances,
+                                                unsigned char* const member_mask,
+                                                double* const maximum_distances,
+                                                int* const member_order,
+                                                int* const sizes,
+                                                const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int row = blockIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    const int seed = seeds[row];
+    member_mask[offset + candidate] = static_cast<unsigned char>(candidate == seed);
+    maximum_distances[offset + candidate] =
+        distances[static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate];
+
+    if (candidate == 0) {
+        sizes[row] = 1;
+        member_order[offset] = seed;
+    }
+}
+
+// For each seed row, find the eligible candidate having the smallest current
+// maximum distance to that row's cluster.  The pair reduction includes the
+// point index, which retains the sequential lower-index tie break exactly.
+__global__ void findClosestPointsKernel(const unsigned char* const clustered,
+                                        const unsigned char* const member_mask,
+                                        const double* const maximum_distances,
+                                        int* const closest_points,
+                                        int* const any_choice,
+                                        const double threshold,
+                                        const int point_count) {
+    const int row = blockIdx.x;
+    const int thread = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+
+    double best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+    int best_candidate = point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = thread; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        if (clustered[candidate] || member_mask[offset + candidate]) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double candidate_distance = maximum_distances[offset + candidate];
+        if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+            (candidate_distance < best_distance ||
+             (candidate_distance == best_distance && candidate < best_candidate))) {
+            best_distance = candidate_distance;
+            best_candidate = candidate;
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double reduced_distances[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int reduced_candidates[CUDA_THREADS];
+    reduced_distances[thread] = best_distance;
+    reduced_candidates[thread] = best_candidate;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int stride = CUDA_THREADS / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (thread < stride) {
+            const double other_distance = reduced_distances[thread + stride];
+            const int other_candidate = reduced_candidates[thread + stride];
+            if (other_distance < reduced_distances[thread] ||
+                (other_distance == reduced_distances[thread] &&
+                 other_candidate < reduced_candidates[thread])) {
+                reduced_distances[thread] = other_distance;
+                reduced_candidates[thread] = other_candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (thread == 0) {
+        const int closest = reduced_candidates[0] == point_count
+                                ? -1
+                                : reduced_candidates[0];
+        closest_points[row] = closest;
+        if (closest >= 0) {
+            atomicExch(any_choice, 1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Add the selected point to every row's candidate cluster.  Updating the
+// per-candidate maxima incrementally is equivalent to rescanning all current
+// members, but replaces that cubic inner work with one distance-matrix load.
+__global__ void advanceCandidateClustersKernel(
+    const int* const closest_points, const double* const distances,
+    unsigned char* const member_mask, double* const maximum_distances,
+    int* const member_order, int* const sizes, const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int row = blockIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int closest = closest_points[row];
+    if (closest < 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    const double new_distance =
+        distances[static_cast<size_t>(closest) * point_count + candidate];
+    const double old_distance = maximum_distances[offset + candidate];
+    maximum_distances[offset + candidate] =
+        new_distance > old_distance ? new_distance : old_distance;
+
+    if (candidate == closest) {
+        const int member_position = sizes[row];
+        member_mask[offset + candidate] = 1;
+        member_order[offset + member_position] = candidate;
+        sizes[row] = member_position + 1;
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+

+namespace {
+
+constexpr int CUDA_THREADS = 256;
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFail(const cudaError_t error, const char* operation,
+                           const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d during %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                        \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_error__ = (operation);                           \
+        if (cuda_error__ != cudaSuccess) {                                      \
+            cudaFail(cuda_error__, #operation, __FILE__, __LINE__);             \
+        }                                                                       \
+    } while (false)
+
+void initializeCudaRuntime() {
+    int device_count = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count));
+    if (device_count == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "QT clustering requires a CUDA-capable GPU.\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+    // Force primary-context creation before the benchmark timer starts.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));
+}
+
+// Every row represents the candidate cluster grown from one seed.  The
+// matrix is filled once and then turns each diameter update into one load.
+// Keeping distances as double precision Euclidean distances preserves the
+// comparisons performed by the sequential implementation.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrixKernel(const Point* const points,
+                                          double* const distances,
+                                          const int point_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t element_count = static_cast<size_t>(point_count) * point_count;
+    if (index >= element_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[row].x - points[column].x;
+    const double dy = points[row].y - points[column].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+// Initialize one independent candidate cluster for every currently
+// unclustered seed.  The member mask and maximum-distance vector are stored
+// row-major so that each warp accesses consecutive candidate points.
+__global__ void initializeCandidateStateKernel(const int* const seeds,
+                                                const double* const distances,
+                                                unsigned char* const member_mask,
+                                                double* const maximum_distances,
+                                                int* const member_order,
+                                                int* const sizes,
+                                                const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int row = blockIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    const int seed = seeds[row];
+    member_mask[offset + candidate] = static_cast<unsigned char>(candidate == seed);
+    maximum_distances[offset + candidate] =
+        distances[static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate];
+
+    if (candidate == 0) {
+        sizes[row] = 1;
+        member_order[offset] = seed;
+    }
+}
+
+// For each seed row, find the eligible candidate having the smallest current
+// maximum distance to that row's cluster.  The pair reduction includes the
+// point index, which retains the sequential lower-index tie break exactly.
+__global__ void findClosestPointsKernel(const unsigned char* const clustered,
+                                        const unsigned char* const member_mask,
+                                        const double* const maximum_distances,
+                                        int* const closest_points,
+                                        int* const any_choice,
+                                        const double threshold,
+                                        const int point_count) {
+    const int row = blockIdx.x;
+    const int thread = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+
+    double best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+    int best_candidate = point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = thread; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        if (clustered[candidate] || member_mask[offset + candidate]) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double candidate_distance = maximum_distances[offset + candidate];
+        if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+            (candidate_distance < best_distance ||
+             (candidate_distance == best_distance && candidate < best_candidate))) {
+            best_distance = candidate_distance;
+            best_candidate = candidate;
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double reduced_distances[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int reduced_candidates[CUDA_THREADS];
+    reduced_distances[thread] = best_distance;
+    reduced_candidates[thread] = best_candidate;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int stride = CUDA_THREADS / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (thread < stride) {
+            const double other_distance = reduced_distances[thread + stride];
+            const int other_candidate = reduced_candidates[thread + stride];
+            if (other_distance < reduced_distances[thread] ||
+                (other_distance == reduced_distances[thread] &&
+                 other_candidate < reduced_candidates[thread])) {
+                reduced_distances[thread] = other_distance;
+                reduced_candidates[thread] = other_candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (thread == 0) {
+        const int closest = reduced_candidates[0] == point_count
+                                ? -1
+                                : reduced_candidates[0];
+        closest_points[row] = closest;
+        if (closest >= 0) {
+            atomicExch(any_choice, 1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Add the selected point to every row's candidate cluster.  Updating the
+// per-candidate maxima incrementally is equivalent to rescanning all current
+// members, but replaces that cubic inner work with one distance-matrix load.
+__global__ void advanceCandidateClustersKernel(
+    const int* const closest_points, const double* const distances,
+    unsigned char* const member_mask, double* const maximum_distances,
+    int* const member_order, int* const sizes, const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int row = blockIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int closest = closest_points[row];
+    if (closest < 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    const double new_distance =
+        distances[static_cast<size_t>(closest) * point_count + candidate];
+    const double old_distance = maximum_distances[offset + candidate];
+    maximum_distances[offset + candidate] =
+        new_distance > old_distance ? new_distance : old_distance;
+
+    if (candidate == closest) {
+        const int member_position = sizes[row];
+        member_mask[offset + candidate] = 1;
+        member_order[offset + member_position] = candidate;
+        sizes[row] = member_position + 1;
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+

+namespace {
+
+constexpr int CUDA_THREADS = 256;
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFail(const cudaError_t error, const char* operation,
+                           const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d during %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                        \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_error__ = (operation);                           \
+        if (cuda_error__ != cudaSuccess) {                                      \
+            cudaFail(cuda_error__, #operation, __FILE__, __LINE__);             \
+        }                                                                       \
+    } while (false)
+
+void initializeCudaRuntime() {
+    int device_count = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count));
+    if (device_count == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "QT clustering requires a CUDA-capable GPU.\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+    // Force primary-context creation before the benchmark timer starts.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));
+}
+
+// Every row represents the candidate cluster grown from one seed.  The
+// matrix is filled once and then turns each diameter update into one load.
+// Keeping distances as double precision Euclidean distances preserves the
+// comparisons performed by the sequential implementation.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrixKernel(const Point* const points,
+                                          double* const distances,
+                                          const int point_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t element_count = static_cast<size_t>(point_count) * point_count;
+    if (index >= element_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[row].x - points[column].x;
+    const double dy = points[row].y - points[column].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+// Initialize one independent candidate cluster for every currently
+// unclustered seed.  The member mask and maximum-distance vector are stored
+// row-major so that each warp accesses consecutive candidate points.
+__global__ void initializeCandidateStateKernel(const int* const seeds,
+                                                const double* const distances,
+                                                unsigned char* const member_mask,
+                                                double* const maximum_distances,
+                                                int* const member_order,
+                                                int* const sizes,
+                                                const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int row = blockIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    const int seed = seeds[row];
+    member_mask[offset + candidate] = static_cast<unsigned char>(candidate == seed);
+    maximum_distances[offset + candidate] =
+        distances[static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate];
+
+    if (candidate == 0) {
+        sizes[row] = 1;
+        member_order[offset] = seed;
+    }
+}
+
+// For each seed row, find the eligible candidate having the smallest current
+// maximum distance to that row's cluster.  The pair reduction includes the
+// point index, which retains the sequential lower-index tie break exactly.
+__global__ void findClosestPointsKernel(const unsigned char* const clustered,
+                                        const unsigned char* const member_mask,
+                                        const double* const maximum_distances,
+                                        int* const closest_points,
+                                        int* const any_choice,
+                                        const double threshold,
+                                        const int point_count) {
+    const int row = blockIdx.x;
+    const int thread = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+
+    double best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+    int best_candidate = point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = thread; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        if (clustered[candidate] || member_mask[offset + candidate]) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double candidate_distance = maximum_distances[offset + candidate];
+        if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+            (candidate_distance < best_distance ||
+             (candidate_distance == best_distance && candidate < best_candidate))) {
+            best_distance = candidate_distance;
+            best_candidate = candidate;
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double reduced_distances[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int reduced_candidates[CUDA_THREADS];
+    reduced_distances[thread] = best_distance;
+    reduced_candidates[thread] = best_candidate;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int stride = CUDA_THREADS / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (thread < stride) {
+            const double other_distance = reduced_distances[thread + stride];
+            const int other_candidate = reduced_candidates[thread + stride];
+            if (other_distance < reduced_distances[thread] ||
+                (other_distance == reduced_distances[thread] &&
+                 other_candidate < reduced_candidates[thread])) {
+                reduced_distances[thread] = other_distance;
+                reduced_candidates[thread] = other_candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (thread == 0) {
+        const int closest = reduced_candidates[0] == point_count
+                                ? -1
+                                : reduced_candidates[0];
+        closest_points[row] = closest;
+        if (closest >= 0) {
+            atomicExch(any_choice, 1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Add the selected point to every row's candidate cluster.  Updating the
+// per-candidate maxima incrementally is equivalent to rescanning all current
+// members, but replaces that cubic inner work with one distance-matrix load.
+__global__ void advanceCandidateClustersKernel(
+    const int* const closest_points, const double* const distances,
+    unsigned char* const member_mask, double* const maximum_distances,
+    int* const member_order, int* const sizes, const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int row = blockIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int closest = closest_points[row];
+    if (closest < 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    const double new_distance =
+        distances[static_cast<size_t>(closest) * point_count + candidate];
+    const double old_distance = maximum_distances[offset + candidate];
+    maximum_distances[offset + candidate] =
+        new_distance > old_distance ? new_distance : old_distance;
+
+    if (candidate == closest) {
+        const int member_position = sizes[row];
+        member_mask[offset + candidate] = 1;
+        member_order[offset + member_position] = candidate;
+        sizes[row] = member_position + 1;
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+

    97	    if (candidate >= point_count) {
    98	        return;
    99	    }
   100	
   101	    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
   102	    const int seed = seeds[row];
   103	    member_mask[offset + candidate] = static_cast<unsigned char>(candidate == seed);
   104	    maximum_distances[offset + candidate] =
   105	        distances[static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate];
   106	
   107	    if (candidate == 0) {
   108	        sizes[row] = 1;
   109	        member_order[offset] = seed;
   110	    }
   111	}
   112	
   113	// For each seed row, find the eligible candidate having the smallest current
   114	// maximum distance to that row's cluster.  The pair reduction includes the
   115	// point index, which retains the sequential lower-index tie break exactly.
   116	__global__ void findClosestPointsKernel(const unsigned char* const clustered,
   117	                                        const unsigned char* const member_mask,
   118	                                        const double* const maximum_distances,
   119	                                        int* const closest_points,
   120	                                        int* const any_choice,
   121	                                        const double threshold,
   122	                                        const int point_count) {
   123	    const int row = blockIdx.x;
   124	    const int thread = threadIdx.x;
   125	    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
   126	
   127	    double best_distance = DBL_MAX;
   128	    int best_candidate = point_count;
   129	
   130	    for (int candidate = thread; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
   131	        if (clustered[candidate] || member_mask[offset + candidate]) {
   132	            continue;
   133	        }
   134	
   135	        const double candidate_distance = maximum_distances[offset + candidate];
   136	        if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
   137	            (candidate_distance < best_distance ||
   138	             (candidate_distance == best_distance && candidate < best_candidate))) {
   139	            best_distance = candidate_distance;
   140	            best_candidate = candidate;
   141	        }
   142	    }
   143	
   144	    __shared__ double reduced_distances[CUDA_THREADS];
   145	    __shared__ int reduced_candidates[CUDA_THREADS];
   146	    reduced_distances[thread] = best_distance;
   147	    reduced_candidates[thread] = best_candidate;
   148	    __syncthreads();
   149	
   150	    for (int stride = CUDA_THREADS / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
   151	        if (thread < stride) {
   152	            const double other_distance = reduced_distances[thread + stride];
   153	            const int other_candidate = reduced_candidates[thread + stride];
   154	            if (other_distance < reduced_distances[thread] ||
   155	                (other_distance == reduced_distances[thread] &&
   156	                 other_candidate < reduced_candidates[thread])) {
   157	                reduced_distances[thread] = other_distance;
   158	                reduced_candidates[thread] = other_candidate;
   159	            }
   160	        }
   161	        __syncthreads();
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    if (thread == 0) {
   165	        const int closest = reduced_candidates[0] == point_count
   166	                                ? -1
   167	                                : reduced_candidates[0];
   168	        closest_points[row] = closest;
   169	        if (closest >= 0) {
   170	            atomicExch(any_choice, 1);
   171	        }
   172	    }
   173	}
   174	
   175	// Add the selected point to every row's candidate cluster.  Updating the
   176	// per-candidate maxima incrementally is equivalent to rescanning all current
   177	// members, but replaces that cubic inner work with one distance-matrix load.
   178	__global__ void advanceCandidateClustersKernel(
   179	    const int* const closest_points, const double* const distances,
   180	    unsigned char* const member_mask, double* const maximum_distances,
   181	    int* const member_order, int* const sizes, const int point_count) {
   182	    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   183	    const int row = blockIdx.y;
   184	    if (candidate >= point_count) {
   185	        return;
   186	    }
   187	
   188	    const int closest = closest_points[row];
   189	    if (closest < 0) {
   190	        return;
   191	    }
   192	
   193	    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
   194	    const double new_distance =
   195	        distances[static_cast<size_t>(closest) * point_count + candidate];
   196	    const double old_distance = maximum_distances[offset + candidate];
   197	    maximum_distances[offset + candidate] =
   198	        new_distance > old_distance ? new_distance : old_distance;
   199	
   200	    if (candidate == closest) {
   201	        const int member_position = sizes[row];
   202	        member_mask[offset + candidate] = 1;
   203	        member_order[offset + member_position] = candidate;
   204	        sizes[row] = member_position + 1;
   205	    }
   206	}
   207	
   208	} // namespace
   209	
   210	// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
   211	void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
   212	    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
   213	    
   214	    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
   215	    int count = 0;
   216	    
   217	    while (count < N) {
   218	        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
   219	        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
   220	        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
   221	        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
   222	        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
   223	        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
   224	        
   225	        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
   226	        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
   227	            group_cnt = N - count;
   228	        }
   229	        
   230	        while (group_cnt > 0) {
   231	            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
   232	            const double r = frand() * R;
   233	            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
   234	            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
   235	            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
   236	            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
   237	            
   238	            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
   239	                continue;
   240	            }
   241	            
   242	            points[count] = {x, y};
   243	            count++;
   244	            group_cnt--;
   245	        }
   246	    }
   247	}
   248	
   249	// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
   250	inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
   251	    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
   252	    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
   253	    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
   254	}

   260	    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);

+constexpr int CUDA_THREADS = 256;
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFail(const cudaError_t error, const char* operation,
+                           const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d during %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                        \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_error__ = (operation);                           \
+        if (cuda_error__ != cudaSuccess) {                                      \
+            cudaFail(cuda_error__, #operation, __FILE__, __LINE__);             \
+        }                                                                       \
+    } while (false)
+
+void initializeCudaRuntime() {
+    int device_count = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count));
+    if (device_count == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "QT clustering requires a CUDA-capable GPU.\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+    // Force primary-context creation before the benchmark timer starts.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));
+}
+
+// Every row represents the candidate cluster grown from one seed.  The
+// matrix is filled once and then turns each diameter update into one load.
+// Keeping distances as double precision Euclidean distances preserves the
+// comparisons performed by the sequential implementation.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrixKernel(const Point* const points,
+                                          double* const distances,
+                                          const int point_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t element_count = static_cast<size_t>(point_count) * point_count;
+    if (index >= element_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[row].x - points[column].x;
+    const double dy = points[row].y - points[column].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+// Initialize one independent candidate cluster for every currently
+// unclustered seed.  The member mask and maximum-distance vector are stored
+// row-major so that each warp accesses consecutive candidate points.
+__global__ void initializeCandidateStateKernel(const int* const seeds,
+                                                const double* const distances,
+                                                unsigned char* const member_mask,
+                                                double* const maximum_distances,
+                                                int* const member_order,
+                                                int* const sizes,
+                                                const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int row = blockIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    const int seed = seeds[row];
+    member_mask[offset + candidate] = static_cast<unsigned char>(candidate == seed);
+    maximum_distances[offset + candidate] =
+        distances[static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate];
+
+    if (candidate == 0) {
+        sizes[row] = 1;
+        member_order[offset] = seed;
+    }
+}
+
+// For each seed row, find the eligible candidate having the smallest current
+// maximum distance to that row's cluster.  The pair reduction includes the
+// point index, which retains the sequential lower-index tie break exactly.
+__global__ void findClosestPointsKernel(const unsigned char* const clustered,
+                                        const unsigned char* const member_mask,
+                                        const double* const maximum_distances,
+                                        int* const closest_points,
+                                        int* const any_choice,
+                                        const double threshold,
+                                        const int point_count) {
+    const int row = blockIdx.x;
+    const int thread = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+
+    double best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+    int best_candidate = point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = thread; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        if (clustered[candidate] || member_mask[offset + candidate]) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double candidate_distance = maximum_distances[offset + candidate];
+        if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+            (candidate_distance < best_distance ||
+             (candidate_distance == best_distance && candidate < best_candidate))) {
+            best_distance = candidate_distance;
+            best_candidate = candidate;
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double reduced_distances[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int reduced_candidates[CUDA_THREADS];
+    reduced_distances[thread] = best_distance;
+    reduced_candidates[thread] = best_candidate;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int stride = CUDA_THREADS / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (thread < stride) {
+            const double other_distance = reduced_distances[thread + stride];
+            const int other_candidate = reduced_candidates[thread + stride];
+            if (other_distance < reduced_distances[thread] ||
+                (other_distance == reduced_distances[thread] &&
+                 other_candidate < reduced_candidates[thread])) {
+                reduced_distances[thread] = other_distance;
+                reduced_candidates[thread] = other_candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (thread == 0) {
+        const int closest = reduced_candidates[0] == point_count
+                                ? -1
+                                : reduced_candidates[0];
+        closest_points[row] = closest;
+        if (closest >= 0) {
+            atomicExch(any_choice, 1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Add the selected point to every row's candidate cluster.  Updating the
+// per-candidate maxima incrementally is equivalent to rescanning all current
+// members, but replaces that cubic inner work with one distance-matrix load.
+__global__ void advanceCandidateClustersKernel(
+    const int* const closest_points, const double* const distances,
+    unsigned char* const member_mask, double* const maximum_distances,
+    int* const member_order, int* const sizes, const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int row = blockIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int closest = closest_points[row];
+    if (closest < 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    const double new_distance =
+        distances[static_cast<size_t>(closest) * point_count + candidate];
+    const double old_distance = maximum_distances[offset + candidate];
+    maximum_distances[offset + candidate] =
+        new_distance > old_distance ? new_distance : old_distance;
+
+    if (candidate == closest) {
+        const int member_position = sizes[row];
+        member_mask[offset + candidate] = 1;
+        member_order[offset + member_position] = candidate;
+        sizes[row] = member_position + 1;
+    }
+}

Implemented unconditional CUDA QT clustering in [qtclustering.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_cuda_r1/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp:65) and CUDA build configuration in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_cuda_r1/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt:1).

- GPU-resident distance matrix and per-seed candidate-cluster state.
- Parallel greedy candidate selection with deterministic lower-index and first-seed tie-breaking.
- Incremental maximum-diameter updates preserve the original algorithm while eliminating repeated member rescans.
- Source is compiled by `nvcc`; executable remains `qtclustering`.

Verified successfully:

- CUDA build completes.
- Membership hashes exactly match the sequential baseline across several valid sizes/thresholds, including 1,000 points.
- `-v` validation passes at 1,000 points.
- On the RTX 3090, 1,000 points ran in 35 ms versus 7,012 ms for the captured sequential baseline.

+namespace {
+
+constexpr int CUDA_THREADS = 256;
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFail(const cudaError_t error, const char* operation,
+                           const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d during %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                        \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_error__ = (operation);                           \
+        if (cuda_error__ != cudaSuccess) {                                      \
+            cudaFail(cuda_error__, #operation, __FILE__, __LINE__);             \
+        }                                                                       \
+    } while (false)
+
+void initializeCudaRuntime() {
+    int device_count = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count));
+    if (device_count == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "QT clustering requires a CUDA-capable GPU.\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+    // Force primary-context creation before the benchmark timer starts.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));
+}
+
+// Every row represents the candidate cluster grown from one seed.  The
+// matrix is filled once and then turns each diameter update into one load.
+// Keeping distances as double precision Euclidean distances preserves the
+// comparisons performed by the sequential implementation.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrixKernel(const Point* const points,
+                                          double* const distances,
+                                          const int point_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t element_count = static_cast<size_t>(point_count) * point_count;
+    if (index >= element_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[row].x - points[column].x;
+    const double dy = points[row].y - points[column].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+// Initialize one independent candidate cluster for every currently
+// unclustered seed.  The member mask and maximum-distance vector are stored
+// row-major so that each warp accesses consecutive candidate points.
+__global__ void initializeCandidateStateKernel(const int* const seeds,
+                                                const double* const distances,
+                                                unsigned char* const member_mask,
+                                                double* const maximum_distances,
+                                                int* const member_order,
+                                                int* const sizes,
+                                                const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int row = blockIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    const int seed = seeds[row];
+    member_mask[offset + candidate] = static_cast<unsigned char>(candidate == seed);
+    maximum_distances[offset + candidate] =
+        distances[static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate];
+
+    if (candidate == 0) {
+        sizes[row] = 1;
+        member_order[offset] = seed;
+    }
+}
+
+// For each seed row, find the eligible candidate having the smallest current
+// maximum distance to that row's cluster.  The pair reduction includes the
+// point index, which retains the sequential lower-index tie break exactly.
+__global__ void findClosestPointsKernel(const unsigned char* const clustered,
+                                        const unsigned char* const member_mask,
+                                        const double* const maximum_distances,
+                                        int* const closest_points,
+                                        int* const any_choice,
+                                        const double threshold,
+                                        const int point_count) {
+    const int row = blockIdx.x;
+    const int thread = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+
+    double best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+    int best_candidate = point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = thread; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        if (clustered[candidate] || member_mask[offset + candidate]) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double candidate_distance = maximum_distances[offset + candidate];
+        if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+            (candidate_distance < best_distance ||
+             (candidate_distance == best_distance && candidate < best_candidate))) {
+            best_distance = candidate_distance;
+            best_candidate = candidate;
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double reduced_distances[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int reduced_candidates[CUDA_THREADS];
+    reduced_distances[thread] = best_distance;
+    reduced_candidates[thread] = best_candidate;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int stride = CUDA_THREADS / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (thread < stride) {
+            const double other_distance = reduced_distances[thread + stride];
+            const int other_candidate = reduced_candidates[thread + stride];
+            if (other_distance < reduced_distances[thread] ||
+                (other_distance == reduced_distances[thread] &&
+                 other_candidate < reduced_candidates[thread])) {
+                reduced_distances[thread] = other_distance;
+                reduced_candidates[thread] = other_candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (thread == 0) {
+        const int closest = reduced_candidates[0] == point_count
+                                ? -1
+                                : reduced_candidates[0];
+        closest_points[row] = closest;
+        if (closest >= 0) {
+            atomicExch(any_choice, 1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Add the selected point to every row's candidate cluster.  Updating the
+// per-candidate maxima incrementally is equivalent to rescanning all current
+// members, but replaces that cubic inner work with one distance-matrix load.
+__global__ void advanceCandidateClustersKernel(
+    const int* const closest_points, const double* const distances,
+    unsigned char* const member_mask, double* const maximum_distances,
+    int* const member_order, int* const sizes, const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int row = blockIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int closest = closest_points[row];
+    if (closest < 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    const double new_distance =
+        distances[static_cast<size_t>(closest) * point_count + candidate];
+    const double old_distance = maximum_distances[offset + candidate];
+    maximum_distances[offset + candidate] =
+        new_distance > old_distance ? new_distance : old_distance;
+
+    if (candidate == closest) {
+        const int member_position = sizes[row];
+        member_mask[offset + candidate] = 1;
+        member_order[offset + member_position] = candidate;
+        sizes[row] = member_position + 1;
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
